are you aiming to live your best life many believe the key to a fantastic life is to continually add more activities

and responsibilities yet in reality the opposite is often beneficial it's about removing the toxic elements from your life

today we'll explore 11 things you should discreetly get rid of to enhance your life once you do

you'll immediately see improvements first up distancing yourself from toxic people whether in friendships or romantic relationships

it's well known that we are influenced by those we spend the most time with yet many struggle to end detrimental relationships

they feel compelled to maintain contact even if it's harmful remember past good times can change

and sometimes you must accept that a relationship has run its course it doesn't matter if you've known someone for a decade

if they're impeding your growth or causing you pain it's time to let them go prioritise your well being

and you don't need to explain your decisions to anyone moreover keeping these endings private maintains everyone's dignity

and aids your healing process so reflect on these matters internally rather than seeking everyone's advice

secondly stop feeling sorry for yourself self pity is one of the most self defeating behaviours

yes life can be tough but dwelling in self pity weakens you offering excuses to fail remember

many who faced harsher conditions have reached their goals not through self victimisation but by harnessing their hardships as motivation

so use your challenges as a stepping stone to success third be mindful about your social media use

social media can be incredibly beneficial yet it's easy to lose oneself and endless scrolling

adversely affecting mental health when using social media don't compare your low points to others highlights

which often leads to dissatisfaction and jealousy limit your social media time use it with a clear purpose like visiting a library

and remember to engage in real world activities that bolster your mental health and allow you to appreciate your own life

without constant comparison fourth address self doubt when pursuing goals it's common to face self doubt

which can undermine your confidence and distance you from your objectives instead of an unfounded belief in yourself

recognize when your self doubt might be justified consider what capabilities the better version of yourself would have

to achieve your goals and work towards developing those abilities this approach helps you overcome self doubt

and achieve your aspirations fifth release any resentment holding onto grudges can hinder your inner peace

while resentment might initially motivate you towards your goals long term it can corrupt your soul utilise the energy from these feelings constructively

then let them go and embrace forgiveness granting yourself true peace and happiness sixth eliminate noise

in today's world we are bombarded with constant noise from news to social media updates

it's essential to sometimes mute this noise take moments for silence enjoy a quiet coffee and give yourself space to think

you'll be amazed at the clarity and solutions that can arise from quiet reflection 7th stop the constant comparisons

comparing ourselves to others can quickly become detrimental leading to feelings of inadequacy

and preventing personal growth recognize that everyone's journey is unique focus on your path and celebrate your own achievements

without measuring them against others 8th avoid engaging in gossip gossiping can seem harmless

but is essentially character assassination before speaking ill of others consider if you want similar things said about you

choose to surround yourself with people who prioritise positive genuine interactions which can elevate your overall life quality

ninth don't wait for the perfect moment whether it's starting a business a family or breaking a habit

the perfect moment is a myth take action now and stop procrastinating 10th don't take yourself too seriously

while it's important to be disciplined also make time for laughter and enjoyment which are essential for a balanced

joyful life finally let go of the past dwelling on past events is unproductive

focus instead on the present and future learn from the past but don't let it hold you back from living in the moment

and making the most of today and remember if you're looking to elevate your life don't forget to support us by hitting the like
